Representation.LinearizeMonoidal.δdefMathlib.RepresentationTheory.Action, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $X$ and $Y$ be actions of the set of sets and $G$. Then $\mathrm{Representation\_\_LinearizeMonoidal\_\_\_u3b4\_}$ applied to $X$ and $Y$ is an intertwining map of $\operatorname{linearize}$ applied to $k$, $G$ and the tensor object of $X$ and $Y$ and $\operatorname{tprod}$ applied to $\operatorname{linearize}$ applied to $k$, $G$ and $X$ and $\operatorname{linearize}$ applied to $k$, $G$ and $Y$.
Formula
$X , Y \in \mathrm{Action} (\mathrm{Set} , G)\vdash \mathrm{Representation\_\_LinearizeMonoidal\_\_\_u3b4\_} (X , Y)\in \operatorname{IntertwiningMap} (\operatorname{linearize} (k , G , \operatorname{tensorObj} (X , Y)), \operatorname{tprod} (\operatorname{linearize} (k , G , X), \operatorname{linearize} (k , G , Y)))$
French
Soient $X$ et $Y$ des actions de l'ensemble d'ensembles et $G$. Alors $\mathrm{Representation\_\_LinearizeMonoidal\_\_\_u3b4\_}$ appliqué à $X$ et $Y$ est une application d'entrelacements de $\operatorname{linearize}$ appliqué à $k$, $G$ et l'objet de tenseurs de $X$ et $Y$ et $\operatorname{tprod}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{linearize}$ appliqué à $k$, $G$ et $X$ et $\operatorname{linearize}$ appliqué à $k$, $G$ et $Y$.

Representation.linearizedefMathlib.RepresentationTheory.Action, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $k$ and $G$ be sets. Let $X$ be an action of the set of sets and $G$. Then $\operatorname{linearize}$ applied to $k$, $G$ and $X$ is a representation of $G$ and the set of monoid algebras of $k$ and $\operatorname{V}$ applied to $X$.
Formula
$k , G \in \mathrm{Set} , X \in \mathrm{Action} (\mathrm{Set} , G)\vdash \operatorname{linearize} (k , G , X)\in \mathrm{Representation} (k , G , \mathrm{MonoidAlgebra} (k , \operatorname{V} (X)))$
French

Mathlib.RepresentationTheory.Basic

RepresentationdefMathlib.RepresentationTheory.Basic, type
Wording
representation of G and V (from the name)
English
Let $k$, $G$ and $V$ be sets. Then a representation of $G$ and $V$ is an element of the function that maps $k$, $G$ and $V$ to the set of monoid homomorphisms of $G$ and the set of linear maps of $V$ and $V$.
Formula
$k , G , V \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{Representation} (k , G , V):= \lambda k , G , V . \mathrm{MonoidHom} (G , \mathrm{LinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (k), V , V))$
French
Soient $k$, $G$ et $V$ des ensembles. Alors une représentation de $G$ et $V$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $k$, $G$ et $V$ à l'ensemble d'homomorphismes de monoïdes de $G$ et l'ensemble d'applications linéaires de $V$ et $V$.
Representation.IsTrivialclassMathlib.RepresentationTheory.Basic, predicate
Wording
x4 is trivial (from the name)
English
Let $x_{4}$ be a representation of $G$ and $V$. Then we can say that $x_{4}$ is trivial.
Formula
$x_{4} \in \mathrm{Representation} (k , G , V)\vdash \operatorname{IsTrivial} (x_{4})\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soit $x_{4}$ une représentation de $G$ et $V$. Alors nous pouvons dire que $x_{4}$ est trivial.

the cochain functor of k and G (from the name)
English
Let $k$ and $G$ be sets. Then the cochain functor of $k$ and $G$ is a functor of the set of representations of $k$ and $G$ and the set of cochain complexes of the set of module categories of $k$ and $\Natural$.
Formula
$k , G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{cochainsFunctor} (k , G)\in \operatorname{Functor} (\mathrm{Rep} (k , G), \mathrm{CochainComplex} (\mathrm{ModuleCat} (k), \Natural))$
French
Soient $k$ et $G$ des ensembles. Alors le foncteur de cochaînes de $k$ et $G$ est un foncteur de l'ensemble de représentations de $k$ et $G$ et l'ensemble de nombres complexes de cochaînes de l'ensemble de catégories de modules de $k$ et $\Natural$.
groupCohomology.cochainsMapdefMathlib.RepresentationTheory.Homological.GroupCohomology.Functoriality, function or constant
Wording
the cochain map of f and x3 (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a monoid homomorphism of $G$ and $H$. Let $x_{3}$ be a homomorphism of the restriction of $f$ and $A$ and $B$. Then the cochain map of $f$ and $x_{3}$ is a homomorphism of the inhomogeneous cochain of $A$ and the inhomogeneous cochain of $B$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{MonoidHom} (G , H), x_{3} \in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{res} (f , A), B)\vdash \operatorname{cochainsMap} (f , x_{3})\in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{inhomogeneousCochains} (A), \operatorname{inhomogeneousCochains} (B))$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes de $G$ et $H$. Soit $x_{3}$ un homomorphisme de la restriction de $f$ et $A$ et $B$. Alors l'application de cochaînes de $f$ et $x_{3}$ est un homomorphisme de la cochaîne inhomogène de $A$ et la cochaîne inhomogène de $B$.

Wording
the map isomorphism of he and #4 (from the name)
English
Let $e$ be a multiplicative equivalence of $G$ and $H$. Let $\mathrm{e'}$ be a linear equivalence of $\operatorname{V}$ applied to $B$ and $\operatorname{V}$ applied to $A$ such that for all elements $g$ of $G$, the composition of $\operatorname{toLinearMap}$ applied to $\mathrm{e'}$ and $\mathrm{Rep\_\_\_u3c1\_\_\_app}$ applied to $B$ and $g$ is equal to the composition of $\mathrm{Rep\_\_\_u3c1\_\_\_app}$ applied to $A$ and $e$ applied to $g$ and $\operatorname{toLinearMap}$ applied to $\mathrm{e'}$. French

Let $k$ and $G$ be sets. Then the isomorphism standard complex of $k$ and $G$ is an isomorphism of the bar complex of $k$ and $G$ and the standard complex of $k$ and $G$.
Formula
$k , G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{isoStandardComplex} (k , G)\in \operatorname{Iso} (\operatorname{barComplex} (k , G), \operatorname{standardComplex} (k , G))$
French
Soient $k$ et $G$ des ensembles. Alors le nombre complexe standard d'isomorphismes de $k$ et $G$ est un isomorphisme du nombre complexe de bar de $k$ et $G$ et le nombre complexe standard de $k$ et $G$.
